## Best date plans

### 2026-09-13 — Pioneer Works Second Sundays + Hometown Bar-B-Que

**Pioneer Works Second Sundays, then a meal.** Barbecue at Hometown Bar-B-Que. It is an 8-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 12 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=159+Pioneer+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11231&destination=454+Van+Brunt+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11231&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://pioneerworks.org/programs/second-sundays-september-2026) · [Restaurant](https://hometownbbq.com/)

### 2026-09-12 — Leland Eating and Drinking House + Rooftop Films: Newlyweeds with Shaka King Q&A

**Dinner, then Rooftop Films: Newlyweeds with Shaka King Q&A.** New American at Leland Eating and Drinking House. It is 1.2 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Rooftop Films: Newlyweeds with Shaka King Q&A starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=755+Dean+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11238&destination=Brooklyn+Ave+%26+Prospect+Pl%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11213&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://rooftopfilms.com/event/newlyweeds/) · [Restaurant](https://www.lelandbrooklyn.com/)

### 2026-09-12 — 16th Annual Fort Greene Park Jazz Festival + Ugly Baby

**16th Annual Fort Greene Park Jazz Festival, then dinner.** Thai at Ugly Baby. It is a 22-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 3 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Washington+Park%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11205&destination=364+Grand+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11211&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.fortgreenepark.org/calendar/jazz-festival-071226) · [Restaurant](https://www.uglybabynyc.com/) · [Reservations](https://resy.com/cities/new-york-ny/venues/ugly-baby)

### 2026-09-14 — Cospa Ramen + Paramount+ Movie Nights: Shakespeare in Love

**Dinner, then Paramount+ Movie Nights: Shakespeare in Love.** Ramen at Cospa Ramen. It is a 19-minute walk from the venue. Doors 5 pm — eat first.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Flatiron+District%2C+New+York%2C+NY&destination=Bryant+Park%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10018&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://bryantpark.org/activities/movie-nights)

### 2026-09-13 — Second Sundays at Pioneer Works + Winner on Franklin

**Second Sundays at Pioneer Works, then a meal.** Bakery and cafe at Winner on Franklin. It is 1.9 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 12 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.

### 2026-09-13 — String Orchestra of Brooklyn: Beethoven in the Park + Leland Eating and Drinking House

**String Orchestra of Brooklyn: Beethoven in the Park, then a meal.** New American at Leland Eating and Drinking House. It is an 18-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 2 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.

## Where to eat

A tighter September 12 dining set: I kept date-bound events, current August/September openings, verified restaurant deals, and reclassified older heatmap items as restaurants instead of new openings where useful.

Dining research: 2026-09-12T10:37:03+00:00

No NYC candidate is retained as Michelin-starred. Eater states the Mexico City original El Califa de León was the world’s first Michelin-starred taqueria in 2024, but that is not a verified Michelin award for the New York location. Kirbee’s source mentions Michelin-related background for Texas restaurants/pitmasters, not Kirbee’s. Official Michelin pages were not used to add any new award claims.

Conditions: Lunch offerings are listed by Hawksmoor for New York Wednesday-Saturday, noon-3 PM; exact prices were not visible in the captured page or the follow-up lunch-menu shell. Source: https://thehawksmoor.com/us/hawksmoor-lunch-offerings/

## Book ahead

#### [Printed Matter’s 2026 NY Art Book Fair](https://www.momaps1.org/en/events/841-printed-matter-s-2026-ny-art-book-fair)

2026-09-24 · 7:00 PM · festival · Advance registration suggested; price not listed · MoMA PS1 · Long Island City · Queens · Score 74.0  
Printed Matter’s NY Art Book Fair brings more than 310 exhibitors in artists’ books, zines, rare books, institutions, and distributors to MoMA PS1 for its 2026 edition.
**Timing:** Fair runs September 24–27; detailed daily hours should be checked on the NYABF site before going.
**Why now:** The fair is outside the seven-day lane but registration is already open for a short September 24–27 run.
**Hot booking alert:** MoMA PS1 says advance registration is strongly suggested because NYABF frequently reaches capacity; the fair is a four-day event ending September 27.
[Official](https://www.momaps1.org/en/events/841-printed-matter-s-2026-ny-art-book-fair) · [Tickets / booking](https://printedmatter.artsvp.com/e8b3ee)
**Sale/deadline:** 2026-09-27

#### [A Few Good Men](https://www.lct.org/)

2026-10-08 · First preview October 8; opening October 29; closing January 10. · theater · Official ticket prices vary · Vivian Beaumont Theater · Lincoln Square · Manhattan · Score 64.0  
Aaron Sorkin’s courtroom drama returns to Broadway starring Bradley Whitford and Tom Blyth, directed by Michael Arden, at Lincoln Center Theater’s Vivian Beaumont Theater.
**Why now:** The Broadway League listing confirms the full October 8–January 10 limited run in the book-ahead window.
**Book soon:** Broadway.org lists the Broadway run through January 10, 2027, and the official ticket link is already active through Telecharge.

2026-10-01 · First preview October 1; opening October 21; runtime 100 minutes with no intermission. · theater · Official ticket prices vary · Imperial Theatre · Theater District · Manhattan · Score 59.0  
Billy Crystal’s 860 is an intimate Broadway play about memory, family, friendship, love, luck, loss, and finding laughter after the Palisades fires destroyed his home.

**Book soon:** Broadway.org lists the production as a limited Broadway run through January 3, 2027, with official tickets through Telecharge.
